def TDIBWIDTHBYTES(bits):
    return ((bits + 31) // 32 * 4)

"""
------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Parameter               |   Range       |   Default             |
|-----------------------------------------------------------------|
| Auto Exposure Target    |   16~235      |   120                 |
| Exposure Gain           |   100~        |   100                 |
| Temp                    |   1000~25000  |   6503                |
| Tint                    |   100~2500    |   1000                |
| LevelRange              |   0~255       |   Low = 0, High = 255 |
| Contrast                |   -255~255    |   0                   |
| Hue                     |   -180~180    |   0                   |
| Saturation              |   0~255       |   128                 |
| Brightness              |   -255~255    |   0                   |
| Gamma                   |   20~180      |   100                 |
| WBGain                  |   -127~127    |   0                   |
------------------------------------------------------------------|
"""

class MiicamResolution:
    def __init__(self, w, h):
        self.width = w
        self.height = h

class MiicamFocusMotor:
    def __init__(self, imax, imin, idef, imaxabs, iminabs, zoneh, zonev):
        self.imax = imax                 # maximum auto focus sensor board positon
        self.imin = imin                 # minimum auto focus sensor board positon
        self.idef = idef                 # conjugate calibration positon
        self.imaxabs = imaxabs           # maximum absolute auto focus sensor board positon, micrometer
        self.iminabs = iminabs           # maximum absolute auto focus sensor board positon, micrometer
        self.zoneh = zoneh               # zone horizontal
        self.zonev = zonev               # zone vertical

class MiicamFrameInfoV3:
    def __init__(self):
        self.width = 0
        self.height = 0
        self.flag = 0                    # MIICAM_FRAMEINFO_FLAG_xxxx
        self.seq = 0                     # frame sequence number
        self.timestamp = 0               # microsecond
        self.shutterseq = 0              # sequence shutter counter
        self.expotime = 0                # expotime
        self.expogain = 0                # expogain
        self.blacklevel = 0              # black level

class MiicamGps:
    def __init__(self):
       self.utcstart = 0    # exposure start time: nanosecond since epoch (00:00:00 UTC on Thursday, 1 January 1970, see https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Unix_time)
       self.utcend = 0      # exposure end time
       self.longitude = 0   # millionth of a degree, 0.000001 degree
       self.latitude = 0
       self.altitude = 0    # millimeter
       self.satellite = 0   # number of satellite
       self.reserved = 0    # not used

class MiicamFrameInfoV4:
    def __init__(self):
        self.v3 = MiicamFrameInfoV3()
        self.reserved = 0
        self.uLum = 0
        self.uFV = 0
        self.timecount = 0
        self.framecount = 0
        self.tricount = 0
        self.gps = MiicamGps()

class MiicamImagePtr:
    def __init__(self):
        self.id = 0                      # 0 is reserved as an invalid id
        self.pixelFormat = 0             # MIICAM_PIXELFORMAT_xxxx
        self.ergb = 0                    # see MIICAM_OPTION_RGB
        self.snapR = False               # see Miicam_SnapR
        self.infoVer = 0                 # MiicamFrameInfo version >= 4
        self.reserved = 0
        self.strideRaw = 0               # stride of RAW, 0 means = image width (no padding)
        self.ptrInfo = None
        self.snapCtx = None
        self.ptrRaw = None               # RAW, see MIICAM_OPTION_IMAGEPTRRAW
        self.ptr8 = None
        self.ptr16 = None
                        
class MiicamFrameInfoV2:
    def __init__(self):
        self.width = 0
        self.height = 0
        self.flag = 0                    # MIICAM_FRAMEINFO_FLAG_xxxx
        self.seq = 0                     # frame sequence number
        self.timestamp = 0               # microsecond

class MiicamModelV2:                    # camera model v2
    def __init__(self, name, flag, maxspeed, preview, still, maxfanspeed, ioctrol, xpixsz, ypixsz, res):
        self.name = name                 # model name, in Windows, we use unicode
        self.flag = flag                 # MIICAM_FLAG_xxx, 64 bits
        self.maxspeed = maxspeed         # number of speed level, same as Miicam_get_MaxSpeed(), the speed range = [0, maxspeed], closed interval
        self.preview = preview           # number of preview resolution, same as Miicam_get_ResolutionNumber()
        self.still = still               # number of still resolution, same as Miicam_get_StillResolutionNumber()
        self.maxfanspeed = maxfanspeed   # maximum fan speed, fan speed range = [0, max], closed interval
        self.ioctrol = ioctrol           # number of input/output control
        self.xpixsz = xpixsz             # physical pixel size in micrometer
        self.ypixsz = ypixsz             # physical pixel size in micrometer
        self.res = res                   # MiicamResolution

class MiicamDeviceV2:
    def __init__(self, displayname, id, model):
        self.displayname = displayname   # display name
        self.id = id                     # unique and opaque id of a connected camera, for Miicam_Open
        self.model = model               # MiicamModelV2

class MiicamSelfTrigger:
    def __init__(self, sensingLeft, sensingTop, sensingWidth, sensingHeight, hThreshold, lThreshold, expoTime, expoGain, hCount, lCount, reserved):
        self.sensingLeft = sensingLeft
        self.sensingTop = sensingTop
        self.sensingWidth = sensingWidth
        self.sensingHeight = sensingHeight  # Sensing Area
        self.hThreshold = hThreshold
        self.lThreshold = lThreshold        # threshold High side, threshold Low side
        self.expoTime = expoTime            # Exposure Time
        self.expoGain = expoGain            # Exposure Gain
        self.hCount = hCount
        self.lCount = lCount                # Count threshold High side, Count threshold Low side, thousandths of Sensing Area
        self.reserved = reserved

class MiicamAFState:
    def __init__(self, AF_Mode, AF_Status, AF_LensAP_Update_Flag, AF_LensManual_Flag, Reserved0, Reserved1):
        self.AF_Mode = AF_Mode
        self.AF_Status = AF_Status
        self.AF_LensAP_Update_Flag = AF_LensAP_Update_Flag  # mark for whether the lens aperture is calibrated
        self.AF_LensManual_Flag = AF_LensManual_Flag        # if true, allows manual operation
        self.Reserved0 = Reserved0
        self.Reserved1 = Reserved1

if sys.platform == 'win32':
    class HRESULTException(OSError):
        def __init__(self, hr):
            OSError.__init__(self, None, ctypes.FormatError(hr).strip(), None)
            self.hr = hr
else:
    class HRESULTException(Exception):
        def __init__(self, hr):
            self.hr = hr

class Miicam:
    class __Resolution(ctypes.Structure):
        _fields_ = [('width', ctypes.c_uint),
                    ('height', ctypes.c_uint)]

    class __RECT(ctypes.Structure):
        _fields_ = [('left', ctypes.c_int),
                    ('top', ctypes.c_int),
                    ('right', ctypes.c_int),
                    ('bottom', ctypes.c_int)]

    class __ModelV2(ctypes.Structure):
        pass

    class __DeviceV2(ctypes.Structure):
        pass

    class __FocusMotor(ctypes.Structure):
        _fields_ = [('imax', ctypes.c_int),                # maximum auto focus sensor board positon
                    ('imin', ctypes.c_int),                # minimum auto focus sensor board positon
                    ('idef', ctypes.c_int),                # conjugate calibration positon
                    ('imaxabs', ctypes.c_int),             # maximum absolute auto focus sensor board positon, micrometer
                    ('iminabs', ctypes.c_int),             # maximum absolute auto focus sensor board positon, micrometer
                    ('zoneh', ctypes.c_int),               # zone horizontal
                    ('zonev', ctypes.c_int)]               # zone vertical

    class __FrameInfoV3(ctypes.Structure):
        _fields_ = [('width', ctypes.c_uint),
                    ('height', ctypes.c_uint),
                    ('flag', ctypes.c_uint),               # MIICAM_FRAMEINFO_FLAG_xxxx
                    ('seq', ctypes.c_uint),                # frame sequence number
                    ('timestamp', ctypes.c_longlong),      # microsecond
                    ('shutterseq', ctypes.c_uint),         # sequence shutter counter
                    ('expotime', ctypes.c_uint),           # expotime
                    ('expogain', ctypes.c_ushort),         # expogain
                    ('blacklevel', ctypes.c_ushort)]       # black level

    class __Gps(ctypes.Structure):
        _fields_ = [('utcstart', ctypes.c_longlong),       # UTC: exposure start time
                    ('utcend', ctypes.c_longlong),         # exposure end time
                    ('longitude', ctypes.c_int),           # millionth of a degree, 0.000001 degree
                    ('latitude', ctypes.c_int),
                    ('altitude', ctypes.c_int),            # millimeter
                    ('satellite', ctypes.c_ushort),        # number of satellite
                    ('reserved', ctypes.c_ushort)]         # not used

    class __FrameInfoV4(ctypes.Structure):
        pass

    class __ImagePtr(ctypes.Structure):
        pass

    class __FrameInfoV2(ctypes.Structure):
        _fields_ = [('width', ctypes.c_uint),
                    ('height', ctypes.c_uint),
                    ('flag', ctypes.c_uint),               # MIICAM_FRAMEINFO_FLAG_xxxx
                    ('seq', ctypes.c_uint),                # frame sequence number
                    ('timestamp', ctypes.c_longlong)]      # microsecond

    class __SelfTrigger(ctypes.Structure):
        _fields_ = [('sensingLeft', ctypes.c_uint),
                    ('sensingTop', ctypes.c_uint),
                    ('sensingWidth', ctypes.c_uint),
                    ('sensingHeight', ctypes.c_uint),      # Sensing Area
                    ('hThreshold', ctypes.c_uint),
                    ('lThreshold', ctypes.c_uint),         # threshold High side, threshold Low side
                    ('expoTime', ctypes.c_uint),           # Exposure Time
                    ('expoGain', ctypes.c_ushort),         # Exposure Gain
                    ('hCount', ctypes.c_ushort),
                    ('lCount', ctypes.c_ushort),           # Count threshold High side, Count threshold Low side, thousandths of Sensing Area
                    ('reserved', ctypes.c_ushort)]

    class __AFState(ctypes.Structure):
        _fields_ = [('AF_Status', ctypes.c_uint),
                    ('AF_LensAP_Update_Flag', ctypes.c_byte),  # mark for whether the lens aperture is calibrated
                    ('AF_LensManual_Flag', ctypes.c_byte),     # if true, allows manual operation
                    ('Reserved0', ctypes.c_byte),
                    ('Reserved1', ctypes.c_byte)]

    if sys.platform == 'win32':
        __EVENT_CALLBACK = ctypes.WINFUNCTYPE(None, ctypes.c_uint, ctypes.py_object)
        __PROGRESS_CALLBACK = ctypes.WINFUNCTYPE(None, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.py_object)
        __HOTPLUG_CALLBACK = ctypes.WINFUNCTYPE(None, ctypes.c_void_p)
        __HISTOGRAM_CALLBACK = ctypes.WINFUNCTYPE(None, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_uint, ctypes.py_object)
    else:
        __EVENT_CALLBACK = ctypes.CFUNCTYPE(None, ctypes.c_uint, ctypes.py_object)
        __PROGRESS_CALLBACK = ctypes.CFUNCTYPE(None, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.py_object)
        __HOTPLUG_CALLBACK = ctypes.CFUNCTYPE(None, ctypes.c_void_p)
        __HISTOGRAM_CALLBACK = ctypes.CFUNCTYPE(None, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_uint, ctypes.py_object)

    __lib = None
    __progress_fun = None
    __progress_ctx = None
    __progress_cb = None
    __hotplug_fun = None
    __hotplug_ctx = None
    __hotplug_cb = None
    __gigeenable_fun = None
    __gigeenable_ctx = None
    __gigeenable_cb = None
    __pcienable_fun = None
    __pcienable_ctx = None
    __pcienable_cb = None
    __ctienable_fun = None
    __ctienable_ctx = None
    __ctienable_cb = None

    @staticmethod
    def __errcheck(result, fun, args):
        if result < 0:
            raise HRESULTException(result)
        return args

    @staticmethod
    def __convertStr(x):
        if isinstance(x, str):
            return x
        else:
            return x.decode('ascii')

    @classmethod
    def Version(cls):
        """get the version of this dll, which is: 60.31488.20260519"""
        cls.__initlib()
        return cls.__lib.Miicam_Version()

    @staticmethod
    def __convertResolution(a):
        t = []
        for i in range(0, a.preview):
            t.append(MiicamResolution(a.res[i].width, a.res[i].height))
        return t

    @staticmethod
    def __convertModel(a):
        t = MiicamModelV2(__class__.__convertStr(a.name), a.flag, a.maxspeed, a.preview, a.still, a.maxfanspeed, a.ioctrol, a.xpixsz, a.ypixsz, __class__.__convertResolution(a))
        return t

    @staticmethod
    def __convertDevice(a):
        return MiicamDeviceV2(__class__.__convertStr(a.displayname), __class__.__convertStr(a.id), __class__.__convertModel(a.model.contents))

    @staticmethod
    def __gigeEnableCallbackFun(ctx):
        if __class__.__gigeenable_fun:
            __class__.__gigeenable_fun(__class__.__gigeenable_ctx)

    @classmethod
    def GigeEnable(cls, fun, ctx):
        """Initialize support for GigE cameras. If online/offline notifications are not required, the callback function can be set to None"""
        cls.GigeEnableV2(fun, ctx, None)

    @classmethod
    def GigeEnableV2(cls, fun, ctx, opt):
        """
        opt: semicolon separated options:
            "wifi": Enable WiFi adapter support
        """
        cls.__initlib()
        cls.__gigeenable_fun = fun
        cls.__gigeenable_ctx = ctx
        if cls.__gigeenable_fun is None:
            if opt is None or len(opt) == 0:
                cls.__lib.Miicam_GigeEnable(cls.__HOTPLUG_CALLBACK(0), None)
            else:
                cls.__lib.Miicam_GigeEnableV2(cls.__HOTPLUG_CALLBACK(0), None, opt)
        else:
            cls.__gigeenable_cb = cls.__HOTPLUG_CALLBACK(cls.__gigeEnableCallbackFun)
            if opt is None or len(opt) == 0:
                cls.__lib.Miicam_GigeEnable(cls.__gigeenable_cb, None)
            else:
                cls.__lib.Miicam_GigeEnableV2(cls.__gigeenable_cb, None, opt)

    @staticmethod
    def __pciEnableCallbackFun(ctx):
        if __class__.__pcienable_fun:
            __class__.__pcienable_fun(__class__.__pcienable_ctx)

    @classmethod
    def PciEnable(cls, fun, ctx):
        """Initialize support for PCIe cameras. If online/offline notifications are not required, the callback function can be set to None"""
        cls.__initlib()
        cls.__pcienable_fun = fun
        cls.__pcienable_ctx = ctx
        if cls.__pcienable_fun is None:
            cls.__lib.Miicam_PciEnable(cls.__HOTPLUG_CALLBACK(0), None)
        else:
            cls.__pcienable_cb = cls.__HOTPLUG_CALLBACK(cls.__pciEnableCallbackFun)
            cls.__lib.Miicam_PciEnable(cls.__pcienable_cb, None)

    @staticmethod
    def __ctiEnableCallbackFun(ctx):
        if __class__.__ctienable_fun:
            __class__.__ctienable_fun(__class__.__ctienable_ctx)

    @classmethod
    def CtiEnable(cls, fun, ctx, ctiPath):
        """
        Initialize support for *.cti cameras. If online/offline notifications are not required, the callback function can be set to None
        * (1) ctiPath = None means all *.cti in GENICAM_GENTL64_PATH/GENICAM_GENTL32_PATH
        * or
        * (2) ctiPath[] = [
                "/full/path/to/file.cti"
                ...
            ]
        """
        if ctiPath is None:
            ctiArray = None
        else:
            arrPath = ctiPath + [None]
            if sys.platform == 'win32':
                arrType = ctypes.c_wchar_p * len(arrPath)
                ctiArray = ctypes.arr_type(*arrPath)
            else:
                arrType = ctypes.c_char_p * len(arrPath)
                ctiArray = ctypes.arr_type(*arrPath)
        cls.__initlib()
        cls.__ctienable_fun = fun
        cls.__ctienable_ctx = ctx
        if cls.__ctienable_fun is None:
            cls.__lib.Miicam_CtiEnable(cls.__HOTPLUG_CALLBACK(0), None, ctiArray)
        else:
            cls.__ctienable_cb = cls.__HOTPLUG_CALLBACK(cls.__ctiEnableCallbackFun)
            cls.__lib.Miicam_CtiEnable(cls.__ctienable_cb, None, ctiArray)

    @staticmethod
    def __hotplugCallbackFun(ctx):
        if __class__.__hotplug_fun:
            __class__.__hotplug_fun(__class__.__hotplug_ctx)

    @classmethod
    def HotPlug(cls, fun, ctx):
        """
        This function is only available on macOS and Linux, it's unnecessary on Windows & Android. To process the device plug in / pull out:
            (1) On Windows, please refer to the MSDN
                (a) Device Management, https://docs.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/devio/device-management
                (b) Detecting Media Insertion or Removal, https://docs.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/devio/detecting-media-insertion-or-removal
            (2) On Android, please refer to https://developer.android.com/guide/topics/connectivity/usb/host
            (3) On Linux / macOS, please call this function to register the callback function.
                When the device is inserted or pulled out, you will be notified by the callback funcion, and then call Miicam_EnumV2(...) again to enum the cameras.
            (4) On macOS, IONotificationPortCreate series APIs can also be used as an alternative.
        """
        if sys.platform == 'win32' or sys.platform == 'android':
            raise HRESULTException(E_NOTIMPL)
        else:
            cls.__initlib()
            cls.__hotplug_fun = fun
            cls.__hotplug_ctx = ctx
            if cls.__hotplug_fun is None:
                cls.__lib.Miicam_HotPlug(cls.__HOTPLUG_CALLBACK(0), None)
            else:
                cls.__hotplug_cb = cls.__HOTPLUG_CALLBACK(cls.__hotplugCallbackFun)
                cls.__lib.Miicam_HotPlug(__hotplug_cb, None)

    @classmethod
    def EnumV2(cls):
        cls.__initlib()
        a = (cls.__DeviceV2 * MIICAM_MAX)()
        n = cls.__lib.Miicam_EnumV2(a)
        arr = []
        for i in range(0, n):
            arr.append(cls.__convertDevice(a[i]))
        return arr

    @classmethod
    def EnumWithName(cls):
        cls.__initlib()
        a = (cls.__DeviceV2 * MIICAM_MAX)()
        n = cls.__lib.Miicam_EnumWithName(a)
        arr = []
        for i in range(0, n):
            arr.append(cls.__convertDevice(a[i]))
        return arr

    def __init__(self, h):
        """the object of Miicam must be obtained by classmethod Open or OpenByIndex, it cannot be obtained by obj = miicam.Miicam()"""
        self.__h = h
        self.__fun = None
        self.__ctx = None
        self.__cb = None
        self.__ctxhistogram = None
        self.__cbhistogram = None

    def __del__(self):
        self.Close()

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *args):
        self.Close()

    def __nonzero__(self):
        return self.__h is not None

    def __bool__(self):
        return self.__h is not None

    @classmethod
    def Open(cls, camId):
        """
        the object of Miicam must be obtained by classmethod Open or OpenByIndex, it cannot be obtained by obj = miicam.Miicam()
        Open(None) means try to Open the first enumerated camera
        For USB, GigE, CameraLink or CXP camera, the camId can also be specified as (case sensitive):
            (a) "sn:xxxxxxxxxxxx" (Use SN, such as sn:ZP250212241204105), or
            (b) "name:xxxxxx" (Use user-defined name, such as name: Camera1)
        Moreover, for GigE camera, the camId can also be specified as (case sensitive):
            (a) "ip:xxx.xxx.xxx.xxx" (Use IP address, such as ip:192.168.1.100), or
            (b) "mac:xxxxxxxxxxxx" (Use MAC address, such as mac:d05f64ffff23)
        """
        cls.__initlib()
        if camId is None:
            h = cls.__lib.Miicam_Open(None)
        elif sys.platform == 'win32':
            h = cls.__lib.Miicam_Open(camId)
        else:
            h = cls.__lib.Miicam_Open(camId.encode('ascii'))
        if h is None:
            return None
        return __class__(h)

    @classmethod
    def OpenByIndex(cls, index):
        """
        the object of Miicam must be obtained by classmethod Open or OpenByIndex, it cannot be obtained by obj = miicam.Miicam()

        the same with Miicam_Open, but use the index as the parameter. such as:
        index == 0, open the first camera,
        index == 1, open the second camera,
        etc
        """
        cls.__initlib()
        h = cls.__lib.Miicam_OpenByIndex(index)
        if h is None:
            return None
        return __class__(h)

    def Close(self):
        if self.__h:
            self.__lib.Miicam_Close(self.__h)
            self.__h = None

    @staticmethod
    def __eventCallbackFun(nEvent, ctx):
        if ctx:
            ctx.__callbackFun(nEvent)

    def __callbackFun(self, nEvent):
        if self.__fun:
            self.__fun(nEvent, self.__ctx)

    def StartPullModeWithCallback(self, fun, ctx):
        self.__fun = fun
        self.__ctx = ctx
        self.__cb = __class__.__EVENT_CALLBACK(__class__.__eventCallbackFun)
        self.__lib.Miicam_StartPullModeWithCallback(self.__h, self.__cb, ctypes.py_object(self))

    @staticmethod
    def __convertFrameInfoV3(pInfo, x):
        pInfo.width = x.width
        pInfo.height = x.height
        pInfo.flag = x.flag
        pInfo.seq = x.seq
        pInfo.shutterseq = x.shutterseq
        pInfo.timestamp = x.timestamp
        pInfo.expotime = x.expotime
        pInfo.expogain = x.expogain
        pInfo.blacklevel = x.blacklevel

    @staticmethod
    def __convertGps(gps, x):
        gps.utcstart = x.utcstart
        gps.utcend = x.utcend
        gps.longitude = x.longitude
        gps.latitude = x.latitude
        gps.altitude = x.altitude
        gps.satellite = x.satellite
        gps.reserved = x.reserved

    @staticmethod
    def __convertFrameInfoV4(pInfo, x):
        __class__.__convertFrameInfoV3(pInfo.v3, x.v3)
        pInfo.reserved = x.reserved
        pInfo.uLum = x.uLum
        pInfo.uFV = x.uFV
        pInfo.timecount = x.timecount
        pInfo.framecount = x.framecount
        pInfo.tricount = x.tricount
        __class__.__convertGps(pInfo.gps, x.gps)

    @staticmethod
    def __convertFrameInfoV2(pInfo, x):
        pInfo.width = x.width
        pInfo.height = x.height
        pInfo.flag = x.flag
        pInfo.seq = x.seq
        pInfo.timestamp = x.timestamp

    @staticmethod
    def __convertImagePtr(ptr):
        newptr = MiicamImagePtr()
        newptr.id = ptr.id                   # 0 is reserved as an invalid id
        newptr.pixelFormat = ptr.pixelFormat # MIICAM_PIXELFORMAT_xxxx
        newptr.ergb = ptr.ergb               # see MIICAM_OPTION_RGB
        newptr.snapR = ptr.snapR             # see Miicam_SnapR
        newptr.infoVer = ptr.infoVer         # MiicamFrameInfo version >= 4
        newptr.reserved = ptr.reserved
        newptr.strideRaw = ptr.strideRaw     # stride of RAW, 0 means = image width (no padding)
        newptr.ptrInfo = ptr.ptrInfo
        newptr.snapCtx = ptr.snapCtx
        newptr.ptrRaw = ptr.ptrRaw           # RAW, see MIICAM_OPTION_IMAGEPTRRAW
        newptr.ptr8 = ptr.ptr8
        newptr.ptr16 = ptr.ptr16
        return newptr

    @classmethod
    def Ptr2FrameInfoV4(cls, ptr):
        info = MiicamFrameInfoV4();
        __class__.__convertFrameInfoV4(info, ctypes.cast(ptr.ptrInfo, ctypes.POINTER(cls.__FrameInfoV4)).contents)
        return info

    @classmethod
    def Ptr2FrameInfoV3(cls, ptr):
        info = MiicamFrameInfoV3();
        __class__.__convertFrameInfoV3(info, ctypes.cast(ptr.ptrInfo, ctypes.POINTER(cls.__FrameInfoV3)).contents)
        return info

    def PullImagePtr(self, bStill):
        """
            Obtains a pointer to the frame buffer directly from the SDK, eliminating the need to copy frame data and thus improving performance
            bStill: to pull still image, set to 1, otherwise 0
        """
        ptr = self.__ImagePtr()
        self.__lib.Miicam_PullImagePtr(self.__h, bStill, ctypes.byref(ptr))
        return __class__.__convertImagePtr(ptr)

    def PushImagePtr(self, ptrId):
        """
            After a frame buffer has been used, it must be returned to the SDK for reuse. Please note the following:
                (a) The frame buffer must only be returned back after it is no longer in use. Any access after returning it is unsafe, as the SDK may have already reused the buffer and overwritten the memory with new data.
                (b) If a frame buffer is not returned, the pool of available buffers will gradually decrease.
                (c) Each frame buffer obtained via Pull (identified by its ID) may be returned only once; duplicate returns are not allowed.
                (d) The return order does not need to match the Pull order; buffers may be returned out of order.
                (e) Frame buffers that have not been returned remain valid after Stop. Frame buffers that have not been returned become invalid immediately after Close.
        """
        self.__lib.Miicam_PushImagePtr(self.__h, ptrId)

    def WaitImagePtr(self, waitMS, bStill):
        """
        waitMS: The timeout interval, in milliseconds. If a nonzero value is specified, the function waits until the image is ok or the interval elapses.
                If waitMS is zero, the function does not enter a wait state if the image is not available; it always returns immediately; this is equal to PullImagePtr.
        """
        ptr = self.__ImagePtr()
        self.__lib.Miicam_WaitImagePtr(self.__h, waitMS, bStill, ctypes.byref(ptr))
        return __class__.__convertImagePtr(ptr)

    """
        waitMS: The timeout interval, in milliseconds. If a non-zero value is specified, the function either successfully fetches the image or waits for a timeout.
                 If waitMS is zero, the function does not wait when there are no images to fetch; It always returns immediately; this is equal to PullImageV4.
        bStill: to pull still image, set to 1, otherwise 0
        bits: 24 (RGB24), 32 (RGB32), 48 (RGB48), 8 (Grey), 16 (Grey), 64 (RGB64).
              In RAW mode, this parameter is ignored.
              bits = 0 means using default bits (see MIICAM_OPTION_RGB).
              When bits and MIICAM_OPTION_RGB are inconsistent, format conversion will have to be performed, resulting in loss of efficiency.
              See the following bits and MIICAM_OPTION_RGB correspondence table:
                ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | MIICAM_OPTION_RGB |   0 (RGB24)   |   1 (RGB48)   |   2 (RGB32)   |   3 (Grey8)   |  4 (Grey16)   |   5 (RGB64)   |
                |--------------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|
                | bits = 0           |      24       |       48      |      32       |       8       |       16      |       64      |
                |--------------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|
                | bits = 24          |      24       |       NA      | Convert to 24 | Convert to 24 |       NA      |       NA      |
                |--------------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|
                | bits = 32          | Convert to 32 |       NA      |       32      | Convert to 32 |       NA      |       NA      |
                |--------------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|
                | bits = 48          |      NA       |       48      |       NA      |       NA      | Convert to 48 | Convert to 48 |
                |--------------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|
                | bits = 8           | Convert to 8  |       NA      | Convert to 8  |       8       |       NA      |       NA      |
                |--------------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|
                | bits = 16          |      NA       | Convert to 16 |       NA      |       NA      |       16      | Convert to 16 |
                |--------------------|---------------|-----------|-------------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|
                | bits = 64          |      NA       | Convert to 64 |       NA      |       NA      | Convert to 64 |       64      |
                |--------------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|

        rowPitch: The distance from one row to the next row. rowPitch = 0 means using the default row pitch. rowPitch = -1 means zero padding, see below:
                ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | format                             | 0 means default row pitch     | -1 means zero padding |
                |------------------------------------|-------------------------------|-----------------------|
                | RGB       | RGB24                  | TDIBWIDTHBYTES(24 * Width)    | Width * 3             |
                |           | RGB32                  | Width * 4                     | Width * 4             |
                |           | RGB48                  | TDIBWIDTHBYTES(48 * Width)    | Width * 6             |
                |           | GREY8                  | TDIBWIDTHBYTES(8 * Width)     | Width                 |
                |           | GREY16                 | TDIBWIDTHBYTES(16 * Width)    | Width * 2             |
                |           | RGB64                  | Width * 8                     | Width * 8             |
                |-----------|------------------------|-------------------------------|-----------------------|
                | RAW       | 8bits Mode             | Width                         | Width                 |
                |           | 10/12/14/16bits Mode   | Width * 2                     | Width * 2             |
                |-----------|------------------------|-------------------------------|-----------------------|
    """
    def PullImageV4(self, pImageData, bStill, bits, rowPitch, pInfo):
        if pInfo is None:
            self.__lib.Miicam_PullImageV4(self.__h, pImageData, bStill, bits, rowPitch, None)
        else:
            x = self.__FrameInfoV4()
            self.__lib.Miicam_PullImageV4(self.__h, pImageData, bStill, bits, rowPitch, ctypes.byref(x))
            self.__convertFrameInfoV4(pInfo, x)

    def WaitImageV4(self, waitMS, pImageData, bStill, bits, rowPitch, pInfo):
        if pInfo is None:
            self.__lib.Miicam_WaitImageV4(self.__h, waitMS, pImageData, bStill, bits, rowPitch, None)
        else:
            x = self.__FrameInfoV4()
            self.__lib.Miicam_WaitImageV4(self.__h, waitMS, pImageData, bStill, bits, rowPitch, ctypes.byref(x))
            self.__convertFrameInfoV4(pInfo, x)

    def PullImageV3(self, pImageData, bStill, bits, rowPitch, pInfo):
        if pInfo is None:
            self.__lib.Miicam_PullImageV3(self.__h, pImageData, bStill, bits, rowPitch, None)
        else:
            x = self.__FrameInfoV3()
            self.__lib.Miicam_PullImageV3(self.__h, pImageData, bStill, bits, rowPitch, ctypes.byref(x))
            self.__convertFrameInfoV3(pInfo, x)

    def WaitImageV3(self, waitMS, pImageData, bStill, bits, rowPitch, pInfo):
        if pInfo is None:
            self.__lib.Miicam_WaitImageV3(self.__h, waitMS, pImageData, bStill, bits, rowPitch, None)
        else:
            x = self.__FrameInfoV3()
            self.__lib.Miicam_WaitImageV3(self.__h, waitMS, pImageData, bStill, bits, rowPitch, ctypes.byref(x))
            self.__convertFrameInfoV3(pInfo, x)

    def PullImageV2(self, pImageData, bits, pInfo):
        if pInfo is None:
            self.__lib.Miicam_PullImageV2(self.__h, pImageData, bits, None)
        else:
            x = self.__FrameInfoV2()
            self.__lib.Miicam_PullImageV2(self.__h, pImageData, bits, ctypes.byref(x))
            self.__convertFrameInfoV2(pInfo, x)

    def PullStillImageV2(self, pImageData, bits, pInfo):
        if pInfo is None:
            self.__lib.Miicam_PullStillImageV2(self.__h, pImageData, bits, None)
        else:
            x = self.__FrameInfoV2()
            self.__lib.Miicam_PullStillImageV2(self.__h, pImageData, bits, ctypes.byref(x))
            self.__convertFrameInfoV2(pInfo, x)

    def PullImageWithRowPitchV2(self, pImageData, bits, rowPitch, pInfo):
        if pInfo is None:
            self.__lib.Miicam_PullImageWithRowPitchV2(self.__h, pImageData, bits, rowPitch, None)
        else:
            x = self.__FrameInfoV2()
            self.__lib.Miicam_PullImageWithRowPitchV2(self.__h, pImageData, bits, rowPitch, ctypes.byref(x))
            self.__convertFrameInfoV2(pInfo, x)

    def PullStillImageWithRowPitchV2(self, pImageData, bits, rowPitch, pInfo):
        if pInfo is None:
            self.__lib.Miicam_PullStillImageWithRowPitchV2(self.__h, pImageData, bits, rowPitch, None)
        else:
            x = self.__FrameInfoV2()
            self.__lib.Miicam_PullStillImageWithRowPitchV2(self.__h, pImageData, bits, rowPitch, ctypes.byref(x))
            self.__convertFrameInfoV2(pInfo, x)

    def ResolutionNumber(self):
        return self.__lib.Miicam_get_ResolutionNumber(self.__h)

    def StillResolutionNumber(self):
        """return (width, height)"""
        return self.__lib.Miicam_get_StillResolutionNumber(self.__h)

    def MonoMode(self):
        return (self.__lib.Miicam_get_MonoMode(self.__h) == 0)

    def MaxSpeed(self):
        """get the maximum speed, 'Frame Speed Level'"""
        return self.__lib.Miicam_get_MaxSpeed(self.__h)

    def MaxBitDepth(self):
        """get the max bitdepth of this camera, such as 8, 10, 12, 14, 16"""
        return self.__lib.Miicam_get_MaxBitDepth(self.__h)

    def FanMaxSpeed(self):
        """get the maximum fan speed, the fan speed range = [0, max], closed interval"""
        return self.__lib.Miicam_get_FanMaxSpeed(self.__h)

    def Revision(self):
        """get the revision"""
        x = ctypes.c_ushort(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_Revision(self.__h, ctypes.byref(x))
        return x.value

    def SerialNumber(self):
        """get the serial number which is always 32 chars which is zero-terminated such as: TP110826145730ABCD1234FEDC56787"""
        str = (ctypes.c_char * 32)()
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_SerialNumber(self.__h, str)
        return str.value.decode('ascii')

    def FwVersion(self):
        """get the camera firmware version, such as: 3.2.1.20140922"""
        str = (ctypes.c_char * 16)()
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_FwVersion(self.__h, str)
        return str.value.decode('ascii')

    def HwVersion(self):
        """get the camera hardware version, such as: 3.2.1.20140922"""
        str = (ctypes.c_char * 16)()
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_HwVersion(self.__h, str)
        return str.value.decode('ascii')

    def ProductionDate(self):
        """such as: 20150327"""
        str = (ctypes.c_char * 16)()
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_ProductionDate(self.__h, str)
        return str.value.decode('ascii')

    def FpgaVersion(self):
        str = (ctypes.c_char * 16)()
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_FpgaVersion(self.__h, str)
        return str.value.decode('ascii')

    def Field(self):
        return self.__lib.Miicam_get_Field(self.__h)

    def Stop(self):
        self.__lib.Miicam_Stop(self.__h)

    def Pause(self, bPause):
        """1 => pause, 0 => continue"""
        self.__lib.Miicam_Pause(self.__h, ctypes.c_int(1 if bPause else 0))

    def Snap(self, nResolutionIndex):
        """still image snap, nResolutionIndex = 0xffffffff means use the cureent preview resolution"""
        self.__lib.Miicam_Snap(self.__h, ctypes.c_uint(nResolutionIndex))

    def SnapN(self, nResolutionIndex, nNumber):
        """multiple still image snap, nResolutionIndex = 0xffffffff means use the cureent preview resolution"""
        self.__lib.Miicam_SnapN(self.__h, ctypes.c_uint(nResolutionIndex), ctypes.c_uint(nNumber))

    def SnapR(self, nResolutionIndex, nNumber):
        """multiple RAW still image snap, nResolutionIndex = 0xffffffff means use the cureent preview resolution"""
        self.__lib.Miicam_SnapR(self.__h, ctypes.c_uint(nResolutionIndex), ctypes.c_uint(nNumber))

    def SnapV2(self, nResolutionIndex, nNumber, eRGB):
        self.__lib.Miicam_SnapV2(self.__h, ctypes.c_uint(nResolutionIndex), ctypes.c_uint(nNumber), ctypes.c_int(eRGB), ctypes.c_void_p(None))

    def Trigger(self, nNumber):
        """
        soft trigger:
        nNumber:    0xffff:     trigger continuously
                    0:          cancel trigger
                    others:     number of images to be triggered
        """
        self.__lib.Miicam_Trigger(self.__h, ctypes.c_ushort(nNumber))

    def TriggerSyncV4(self, waitMS, pImageData, bits, rowPitch, pInfo):
        """
        trigger synchronously
        waitMS:    0:              by default, exposure * 102% + 4000 milliseconds
                    0xffffffff:     wait infinite
                    other:          milliseconds to wait
        """
        if pInfo is None:
            self.__lib.Miicam_TriggerSyncV4(self.__h, waitMS, pImageData, bits, rowPitch, None)
        else:
            x = self.__FrameInfoV4()
            self.__lib.Miicam_TriggerSyncV4(self.__h, waitMS, pImageData, bits, rowPitch, ctypes.byref(x))
            self.__convertFrameInfoV4(pInfo, x)

    def TriggerSync(self, waitMS, pImageData, bits, rowPitch, pInfo):
        """
        trigger synchronously
        waitMS:    0:              by default, exposure * 102% + 4000 milliseconds
                    0xffffffff:     wait infinite
                    other:          milliseconds to wait
        """
        if pInfo is None:
            self.__lib.Miicam_TriggerSync(self.__h, waitMS, pImageData, bits, rowPitch, None)
        else:
            x = self.__FrameInfoV3()
            self.__lib.Miicam_TriggerSync(self.__h, waitMS, pImageData, bits, rowPitch, ctypes.byref(x))
            self.__convertFrameInfoV3(pInfo, x)

    def put_Size(self, nWidth, nHeight):
        self.__lib.Miicam_put_Size(self.__h, ctypes.c_int(nWidth), ctypes.c_int(nHeight))

    def get_Size(self):
        """return (width, height)"""
        x = ctypes.c_int(0)
        y = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_Size(self.__h, ctypes.byref(x), ctypes.byref(y))
        return (x.value, y.value)

    def put_eSize(self, nResolutionIndex):
        """
        put_Size, put_eSize, can be used to set the video output resolution BEFORE Start.
        put_Size use width and height parameters, put_eSize use the index parameter.
        for example, UCMOS03100KPA support the following resolutions:
            index 0:    2048,   1536
            index 1:    1024,   768
            index 2:    680,    510
        so, we can use put_Size(h, 1024, 768) or put_eSize(h, 1). Both have the same effect.
        """
        self.__lib.Miicam_put_eSize(self.__h, ctypes.c_uint(nResolutionIndex))

    def get_eSize(self):
        x = ctypes.c_uint(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_eSize(self.__h, ctypes.byref(x))
        return x.value

    def get_FinalSize(self):
        """final size after ROI, rotate, binning"""
        x = ctypes.c_int(0)
        y = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_FinalSize(self.__h, ctypes.byref(x), ctypes.byref(y))
        return (x.value, y.value)

    def get_Resolution(self, nResolutionIndex):
        """return (width, height)"""
        x = ctypes.c_int(0)
        y = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_Resolution(self.__h, ctypes.c_uint(nResolutionIndex), ctypes.byref(x), ctypes.byref(y))
        return (x.value, y.value)

    def get_PixelSize(self, nResolutionIndex):
        """get the sensor pixel size, such as: 2.4um x 2.4um"""
        x = ctypes.c_float(0)
        y = ctypes.c_float(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_PixelSize(self.__h, ctypes.c_uint(nResolutionIndex), ctypes.byref(x), ctypes.byref(y))
        return (x.value, y.value)

    def get_ResolutionRatio(self, nResolutionIndex):
        """numerator/denominator, such as: 1/1, 1/2, 1/3"""
        x = ctypes.c_int(0)
        y = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_ResolutionRatio(self.__h, ctypes.c_uint(nResolutionIndex), ctypes.byref(x), ctypes.byref(y))
        return (x.value, y.value)

    def get_RawFormat(self):
        """
        see: http://www.fourcc.org
        FourCC:
            MAKEFOURCC('G', 'B', 'R', 'G'), see http://www.siliconimaging.com/RGB%20Bayer.htm
            MAKEFOURCC('R', 'G', 'G', 'B')
            MAKEFOURCC('B', 'G', 'G', 'R')
            MAKEFOURCC('G', 'R', 'B', 'G')
            MAKEFOURCC('Y', 'Y', 'Y', 'Y'), monochromatic sensor
            MAKEFOURCC('Y', '4', '1', '1'), yuv411
            MAKEFOURCC('V', 'U', 'Y', 'Y'), yuv422
            MAKEFOURCC('U', 'Y', 'V', 'Y'), yuv422
            MAKEFOURCC('Y', '4', '4', '4'), yuv444
            MAKEFOURCC('R', 'G', 'B', '8'), RGB888
        """
        x = ctypes.c_uint(0)
        y = ctypes.c_uint(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_RawFormat(self.__h, ctypes.byref(x), ctypes.byref(y))
        return (x.value, y.value)

    def put_RealTime(self, val):
        """
        0: no realtime
            stop grab frame when frame buffer deque is full, until the frames in the queue are pulled away and the queue is not full
        1: realtime
            use minimum frame buffer. When new frame arrive, drop all the pending frame regardless of whether the frame buffer is full.
            If DDR present, also limit the DDR frame buffer to only one frame.
        2: soft realtime
            use minimum frame buffer. When new frame arrive, drop all the pending frame regardless of whether the frame buffer is full.
            If DDR present, the DDR frame buffer unchanged.
        default: 0
        """
        self.__lib.Miicam_put_RealTime(self.__h, val)

    def get_RealTime(self):
        b = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_RealTime(self.__h, b)
        return b.value

    def Flush(self):
        """Flush is obsolete, recommend using put_Option(h, MIICAM_OPTION_FLUSH, 3)"""
        self.__lib.Miicam_Flush(self.__h)

    def get_AutoExpoEnable(self):
        """
        mode:
           0: disable auto exposure
           1: auto exposure continue mode
           2: auto exposure once mode

    def put_AutoExpoRange(self, maxTime, minTime, maxGain, minGain):
        """set the maximum/minimal auto exposure time and agin. The default maximum auto exposure time is 350ms"""
        return self.__lib.Miicam_put_AutoExpoRange(self.__h, ctypes.c_uint(maxTime), ctypes.c_uint(minTime), ctypes.c_ushort(maxGain), ctypes.c_ushort(minGain))

    def get_AutoExpoRange(self):
        maxTime = ctypes.c_uint(0)
        minTime = ctypes.c_uint(0)
        maxGain = ctypes.c_ushort(0)
        minGain = ctypes.c_ushort(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_AutoExpoRange(self.__h, ctypes.byref(maxTime), ctypes.byref(minTime), ctypes.byref(maxGain), ctypes.byref(minGain))
        return (maxTime.value, minTime.value, maxGain.value, minGain.value)

    def put_MaxAutoExpoTimeAGain(self, maxTime, maxGain):

    def put_LevelRange(self, aLow, aHigh):
        if len(aLow) == 4 and len(aHigh) == 4:
            x = (ctypes.c_ushort * 4)(aLow[0], aLow[1], aLow[2], aLow[3])
            y = (ctypes.c_ushort * 4)(aHigh[0], aHigh[1], aHigh[2], aHigh[3])
            self.__lib.Miicam_put_LevelRange(self.__h, x, y)
        else:
            raise HRESULTException(E_INVALIDARG)

    def get_LevelRange(self):
        x = (ctypes.c_ushort * 4)()
        y = (ctypes.c_ushort * 4)()
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_LevelRange(self.__h, x, y)
        aLow = (x[0], x[1], x[2], x[3])
        aHigh = (y[0], y[1], y[2], y[3])
        return (aLow, aHigh)

    def put_LevelRangeV2(self, mode, roiX, roiY, roiWidth, roiHeight, aLow, aHigh):
        if len(aLow) == 4 and len(aHigh) == 4:
            x = (ctypes.c_ushort * 4)(aLow[0], aLow[1], aLow[2], aLow[3])
            y = (ctypes.c_ushort * 4)(aHigh[0], aHigh[1], aHigh[2], aHigh[3])
            rc = self.__RECT()
            rc.left = roiX
            rc.right = roiX + roiWidth
            rc.top = roiY
            rc.bottom = roiY + roiHeight
            self.__lib.Miicam_put_LevelRangeV2(self.__h, mode, ctypes.byref(rc), x, y)
        else:
            raise HRESULTException(E_INVALIDARG)

    def get_LevelRangeV2(self):
        mode = ctypes.c_ushort(0)
        x = (ctypes.c_ushort * 4)()
        y = (ctypes.c_ushort * 4)()
        rc = self.__RECT()
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_LevelRange(self.__h, mode, ctypes.byref(rc), x, y)
        aLow = (x[0], x[1], x[2], x[3])
        aHigh = (y[0], y[1], y[2], y[3])
        return (mode, (rc.left, rc.top, rc.right - rc.left, rc.bottom - rc.top), aLow, aHigh)

    def put_WhiteBalanceGain(self, aGain):
        """White Balance, RGB Gain Mode"""
        if len(aGain) == 3:

            self.__lib.Miicam_put_WhiteBalanceGain(self.__h, x)
        else:
            raise HRESULTException(E_INVALIDARG)

        return (x[0], x[1], x[2])

    def put_AWBAuxRect(self, X, Y, Width, Height):
        """Minimum width & height: 4"""
        rc = self.__RECT()
        rc.left = X
        rc.right = X + Width
        rc.top = Y
        rc.bottom = Y + Height
        self.__lib.Miicam_put_AWBAuxRect(self.__h, ctypes.byref(rc))

    def get_AWBAuxRect(self):
        """return (left, top, width, height)"""
        rc = self.__RECT()
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_AWBAuxRect(self.__h, ctypes.byref(rc))
        return (rc.left, rc.top, rc.right - rc.left, rc.bottom - rc.top)

    def put_LEDState(self, iLed, iState, iPeriod):
        """
        led state:
            iLed: Led index, (0, 1, 2, ...)
            iState: 1 => Ever bright; 2 => Flashing; other => Off
            iPeriod: Flashing Period (>= 500ms)
        """
        self.__lib.Miicam_put_LEDState(self.__h, ctypes.c_ushort(iLed), ctypes.c_ushort(iState), ctypes.c_ushort(iPeriod))

    def write_EEPROM(self, addr, pBuffer):
        self.__lib.Miicam_write_EEPROM(self.__h, addr, pBuffer, ctypes.c_uint(len(pBuffer)))

    def read_EEPROM(self, addr, pBuffer):
        self.__lib.Miicam_read_EEPROM(self.__h, addr, pBuffer, ctypes.c_uint(len(pBuffer)))

    def rwc_Flash(self, action, addr, pData):
        """
        action = (zone << 24) | MIICAM_FLASH_XXXX: read, write, erase, query total size, query read/write block size, query erase block size
        addr = address
        """
        self.__lib.Miicam_rwc_Flash(self.__h, action, addr, ctypes.c_uint(len(pData)), pData)

        self.__lib.Miicam_write_Pipe(self.__h, pipeId, pBuffer, ctypes.c_uint(len(pBuffer)))

    def write_UART(self, pBuffer):
        self.__lib.Miicam_write_UART(self.__h, pBuffer, ctypes.c_uint(len(pBuffer)))

    def read_UART(self, pBuffer):
        self.__lib.Miicam_read_UART(self.__h, pBuffer, ctypes.c_uint(len(pBuffer)))

    def put_Option(self, iOption, iValue):
        self.__lib.Miicam_put_Option(self.__h, ctypes.c_uint(iOption), ctypes.c_int(iValue))

    def get_Option(self, iOption):
        x = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_Option(self.__h, ctypes.c_uint(iOption), ctypes.byref(x))
        return x.value

    def get_all_PixelFormatSupport(self):
        num = self.get_PixelFormatSupport(0xff)
        if num <= 0:
            return []
        return [self.get_PixelFormatSupport(i) for i in range(num)]

    def get_PixelFormatSupport(self, cmd):
        """
        cmd:
           0xff:     query the number
           0~number: query the nth pixel format
        output: MIICAM_PIXELFORMAT_xxxx
        """
        x = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_PixelFormatSupport(self.__h, cmd, ctypes.byref(x))
        return x.value

    def put_Binning(self, Value, Method):
        """
        Hardware Binning
        Value: 1x1, 2x2, etc
        Method: Average, Add, Skip
        """
        self.__lib.Miicam_put_Binning(self.__h, Value, Method)

    def get_Binning(self):
        pValue = ctypes.c_char_p(0)
        pMethod = ctypes.c_char_p(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_Binning(self.__h, ctypes.byref(pValue), ctypes.byref(pMethod))
        return (pValue.value.decode('ascii'), pMethod.value.decode('ascii'))

    def get_BinningValue(self):
        num = self.get_BinningNumber()
        if num <= 0:
            return []
        return [self.get_BinningValue(i) for i in range(num)]

    def get_BinningNumber(self):
        return self.__lib.Miicam_get_BinningNumber(self.__h)

    def get_BinningValue(self, index):
        p = ctypes.c_char_p(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_BinningValue(self.__h, index, ctypes.byref(p))
        return p.value.decode('ascii')

    def get_all_BinningMethod(self):
        methods = []
        index = 0
        while True:
            try:
                m = self.get_BinningMethod(index)
                methods.append(m)
                index += 1
            except Exception:
                break
        return methods

    def get_BinningMethod(self, index):
        p = ctypes.c_char_p(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_BinningMethod(self.__h, index, ctypes.byref(p))
        return p.value.decode('ascii')

    def put_Linear(self, v8, v16):
        self.__lib.Miicam_put_Linear(self.__h, v8, v16)

    def put_Curve(self, v8, v16):
        self.__lib.Miicam_put_Curve(self.__h, v8, v16)

    def put_ColorMatrix(self, v):
        if len(v) == 9:
            a = (ctypes.c_double * 9)(v[0], v[1], v[2], v[3], v[4], v[5], v[6], v[7], v[8])
            return self.__lib.Miicam_put_ColorMatrix(self.__h, v)
        else:
            raise HRESULTException(E_INVALIDARG)

    def put_InitWBGain(self, v):
        if len(v) == 3:
            a = (ctypes.c_short * 3)(v[0], v[1], v[2])
            self.__lib.Miicam_put_InitWBGain(self.__h, a)
        else:
            raise HRESULTException(E_INVALIDARG)

    def get_TecTargetRange(self):
        """TEC Target range: [min, max]"""
        x = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_Option(self.__h, MIICAM_OPTION_TECTARGET_RANGE, ctypes.byref(x))
        low, high = x.value & 0xffff, (x.value >> 16) & 0xffff
        if low > 32767:
            low = low - 65536
        if high > 32767:
            high = high - 65536
        return (low, high)

    def get_Temperature(self):
        """get the temperature of the sensor, in 0.1 degrees Celsius (32 means 3.2 degrees Celsius, -35 means -3.5 degree Celsius)"""
        x = ctypes.c_short(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_Temperature(self.__h, ctypes.byref(x))
        return x.value

    def put_Temperature(self, nTemperature):
        """
        set the target temperature of the sensor or TEC, in 0.1 degrees Celsius (32 means 3.2 degrees Celsius, -35 means -3.5 degree Celsius)
        set "-2730" or below means using the default value of this model
        """
        self.__lib.Miicam_put_Temperature(self.__h, ctypes.c_short(nTemperature))

    def put_Roi(self, xOffset, yOffset, xWidth, yHeight):
        """
        xOffset, yOffset, xWidth, yHeight: must be even numbers
        Minimum width & height: 8
        """
        self.__lib.Miicam_put_Roi(self.__h, ctypes.c_uint(xOffset), ctypes.c_uint(yOffset), ctypes.c_uint(xWidth), ctypes.c_uint(yHeight))

    def get_Roi(self):
        """return (xOffset, yOffset, xWidth, yHeight)"""
        x = ctypes.c_uint(0)
        y = ctypes.c_uint(0)
        w = ctypes.c_uint(0)
        h = ctypes.c_uint(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_Roi(self.__h, ctypes.byref(x), ctypes.byref(y), ctypes.byref(w), ctypes.byref(h))
        return (x.value, y.value, w.value, h.value)

    def put_RoiN(self, xOffset, yOffset, xWidth, yHeight):
        """multiple Roi"""
        if len(xOffset) != len(yOffset) or len(xOffset) != len(xWidth) or len(xOffset) != len(yHeight):
            raise HRESULTException(E_INVALIDARG)
        Num = len(xOffset)
        pxOffset = (ctypes.c_uint * Num)(*xOffset)
        pyOffset = (ctypes.c_uint * Num)(*yOffset)
        pxWidth = (ctypes.c_uint * Num)(*xWidth)
        pyHeight = (ctypes.c_uint * Num)(*yHeight)
        self.__lib.Miicam_put_RoiN(self.__h, pxOffset, pyOffset, pxWidth, pyHeight, Num)

    def readPtr(self, key, length):
        if length is None:
            raise HRESULTException(E_INVALIDARG)

        buf = (ctypes.c_ubyte * length)()
        self.__lib.Miicam_readPtr(self.__h, key.encode('ascii'), length, ctypes.byref(buf))
        return bytes(buf)

    def readInt(self, key):
        return int.from_bytes(self.readPtr(key, 4), sys.byteorder, signed=True)

    def readLong(self, key):
        return int.from_bytes(self.readPtr(key, 8), sys.byteorder, signed=True)

    def readString(self, key, length):
        data = self.readPtr(key, length)
        try:
            zero_index = data.index(0)
            data = data[:zero_index]
        except ValueError:
            pass
        return data.decode()

    def writePtr(self, key, data):
        if data is None:
            self.__lib.Miicam_writePtr(self.__h, key.encode('ascii'), 0, None)
        elif isinstance(data, bytes):
            length = len(data)
            if length == 0:
                self.__lib.Miicam_writePtr(self.__h, key.encode('ascii'), 0, None)
            else:
                buf = (ctypes.c_ubyte * length).from_buffer_copy(data)
                self.__lib.Miicam_writePtr(self.__h, key.encode('ascii'), length, ctypes.byref(buf))
        elif isinstance(data, str):
            return self.writePtr(key, data.encode())
        elif isinstance(data, int):
            buf = ctypes.c_int(data)
            self.__lib.Miicam_writePtr(self.__h, key.encode('ascii'), ctypes.sizeof(buf), ctypes.byref(buf))
        else:
            raise HRESULTException(E_INVALIDARG)

    def put_XY(self, x, y):
        self.__lib.Miicam_put_XY(self.__h, ctypes.c_int(x), ctypes.c_int(y))

    def put_SelfTrigger(self, pSt):
        x = self.__SelfTrigger()
        x.sensingLeft = pSt.sensingLeft
        x.sensingTop = pSt.sensingTop
        x.sensingWidth = pSt.sensingWidth
        x.sensingHeight = pSt.sensingHeight
        x.hThreshold = pSt.hThreshold
        x.lThreshold = pSt.lThreshold
        x.expoTime = pSt.expoTime
        x.expoGain = pSt.expoGain
        x.hCount = pSt.hCount
        x.lCount = pSt.lCount
        x.reserved = pSt.reserved
        self.__lib.Miicam_put_SelfTrigger(self.__h, ctypes.byref(x))

    def get_SelfTrigger(self, pSt):
        x = self.__SelfTrigger()
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_SelfTrigger(self.__h, ctypes.byref(x))
        return MiicamSelfTrigger(x.sensingLeft.value, x.sensingTop.value, x.sensingWidth.value, x.sensingHeight.value, x.hThreshold.value, x.lThreshold.value, x.expoTime.value, x.expoGain.value, x.hCount.value, x.lCount.value, x.reserved.value)

    def get_AFState(self):
        x = ctypes.c_uint(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_AFState(self.__h, ctypes.byref(x))
        return x.value

    def put_AFMode(self, mode, bFixedWD, uiNear, uiFar):

    def put_AFRoi(self, xOffset, yOffset, xWidth, yHeight):
        self.__lib.Miicam_put_AFRoi(self.__h, ctypes.c_uint(xOffset), ctypes.c_uint(yOffset), ctypes.c_uint(xWidth), ctypes.c_uint(yHeight))

    def put_AFAperture(self, iAperture):

    def put_AFFMPos(self, iFMPos):

    def get_FrameRate(self):
        """
        get the actual frame rate of the camera at the most recent time (about a few seconds):
        return (Frame, Time, TotalFrame)
        framerate (fps) = Frame * 1000.0 / Time
        """
        x = ctypes.c_uint(0)
        y = ctypes.c_uint(0)
        z = ctypes.c_uint(0)
        self.__lib.Miicam_get_FrameRate(self.__h, ctypes.byref(x), ctypes.byref(y), ctypes.byref(z))
        return (x.value, y.value, z.value)

    def LevelRangeAuto(self):
        self.__lib.Miicam_LevelRangeAuto(self.__h)

    def AwbOnce(self):

    def AwbOnePush(self):

    def IoControl(self, ioLine, eType, outVal):

    def get_FocusMotor(self):

    def set_Name(self, name):
        self.__lib.Miicam_set_Name(self.__h, name.encode())

    def query_Name(self):
        str = (ctypes.c_char * 64)()
        self.__lib.Miicam_query_Name(self.__h, str)
        return str.value.decode()

    @staticmethod
    def __histogramCallbackFun(aHist, nFlag, ctx):
        if ctx:
            ctx.__histogramFun(aHist, nFlag)

    def __histogramFun(self, aHist, nFlag):
        if self.__funhistogram:
            arraySize = 1 << (nFlag & 0x0f)
            if nFlag & 0x8000 == 0:
                arraySize *= 3
            self.__funhistogram(aHist, self.__ctxhistogram)

    def GetHistogram(self, fun, ctx):
        self.__funhistogram = fun
        self.__ctxhistogram = ctx
        self.__cbhistogram = __class__.__HISTOGRAM_CALLBACK(__class__.__histogramCallbackFun)
        self.__lib.Miicam_GetHistogramV2(self.__h, self.__cbhistogram, ctypes.py_object(self))

    @classmethod
    def put_Name(cls, camId, name):
        cls.__initlib()
        if sys.platform == 'win32':
            return cls.__lib.Miicam_put_Name(camId, name)
        else:
            return cls.__lib.Miicam_put_Name(camId.encode('ascii'), name)

    @classmethod
    def get_Name(cls, camId):
        cls.__initlib()
        str = (ctypes.c_char * 64)()
        if sys.platform == 'win32':
            cls.__lib.Miicam_get_Name(camId, str)
        else:
            cls.__lib.Miicam_get_Name(camId.encode('ascii'), str)
        return str.value.decode()

    @classmethod
    def PixelFormatName(cls, pixelFormat):
        cls.__initlib()
        return cls.__lib.Miicam_get_PixelFormatName(pixelFormat).decode('ascii')

    @classmethod
    def PixelFormatBitdepth(cls, pixelFormat):
        cls.__initlib()
        return cls.__lib.Miicam_get_PixelFormatBitdepth(pixelFormat)

    @classmethod
    def Replug(cls, camId):
        """
        simulate replug:
        return > 0, the number of device has been replug
        return = 0, no device found
        return E_ACCESSDENIED if without UAC Administrator privileges
        for each device found, it will take about 3 seconds
        """
        cls.__initlib()
        if sys.platform == 'win32':
            return cls.__lib.Miicam_Replug(camId)
        else:
            return cls.__lib.Miicam_Replug(camId.encode('ascii'))

    @staticmethod
    def __progressCallbackFun(percent, ctx):
        if __class__.__progress_fun:
            __class__.__progress_fun(percent, __progress_ctx)

    @classmethod
    def Update(cls, camId, filePath, pFun, pCtx):
        """
        firmware update:
           camId: camera ID
           filePath: ufw file full path
           pFun: progress percent callback
        Please do not unplug the camera or lost power during the upgrade process, this is very very important.
        Once an unplugging or power outage occurs during the upgrade process, the camera will no longer be available and can only be returned to the factory for repair.
        """
        cls.__initlib()
        cls.__progress_fun = pFun
        cls.__progress_ctx = pCtx
        cls.__progress_cb = cls.__PROGRESS_CALLBACK(cls.__progressCallbackFun)
        if sys.platform == 'win32':
            return cls.__lib.Miicam_Update(camId, filePath, __progress_cb, None)
        else:
            return cls.__lib.Miicam_Update(camId.encode('ascii'), filePath.encode('ascii'), __progress_cb, None)

    @classmethod
    def Gain2TempTint(cls, gain):
        cls.__initlib()
        if len(gain) == 3:
            x = (ctypes.c_int * 3)(gain[0], gain[1], gain[2])
            temp = ctypes.c_int(MIICAM_TINT_DEF)
            tint = ctypes.c_int(MIICAM_TINT_DEF)
            cls.__lib.Miicam_Gain2TempTint(x, ctypes.byref(temp), ctypes.byref(tint))
            return (temp.value, tint.value)
        else:
            raise HRESULTException(E_INVALIDARG)

    @classmethod
    def TempTint2Gain(cls, temp, tint):
        cls.__initlib()
        x = (ctypes.c_int * 3)()
        cls.__lib.Miicam_TempTint2Gain(ctypes.c_int(temp), ctypes.c_int(tint), x)
        return (x[0], x[1], x[2])

    @classmethod
    def __initlib(cls):
        if cls.__lib is None:
            try: # First, try to load the library in the directory where this file is located
                dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.realpath(__file__))
                if sys.platform == 'win32':
                    cls.__lib = ctypes.windll.LoadLibrary(os.path.join(dir, 'miicam.dll'))
                elif sys.platform.startswith('linux'):
                    cls.__lib = ctypes.cdll.LoadLibrary(os.path.join(dir, 'libmiicam.so'))
                else:
                    cls.__lib = ctypes.cdll.LoadLibrary(os.path.join(dir, 'libmiicam.dylib'))
            except OSError:
                pass

            if cls.__lib is None:
                if sys.platform == 'win32':
                    cls.__lib = ctypes.windll.LoadLibrary('miicam.dll')
                elif sys.platform.startswith('linux'):
                    cls.__lib = ctypes.cdll.LoadLibrary('libmiicam.so')
                else:
                    cls.__lib = ctypes.cdll.LoadLibrary('libmiicam.dylib')

            cls.__FrameInfoV4._fields_ = [
                        ('v3', cls.__FrameInfoV3),
                        ('reserved', ctypes.c_uint),
                        ('uLum', ctypes.c_uint),
                        ('uFV', ctypes.c_longlong),
                        ('timecount', ctypes.c_longlong),
                        ('framecount', ctypes.c_uint),
                        ('tricount', ctypes.c_uint),
                        ('gps', cls.__Gps)]
                        
            cls.__ImagePtr._fields_ = [
                        ("id", ctypes.c_uint),
                        ("pixelFormat", ctypes.c_ubyte),
                        ("ergb", ctypes.c_ubyte),
                        ("snapR", ctypes.c_ubyte),
                        ("infoVer", ctypes.c_ubyte),
                        ("reserved", ctypes.c_uint),
                        ("strideRaw", ctypes.c_uint),
                        ("ptrInfo", ctypes.c_void_p),
                        ("snapCtx", ctypes.c_void_p),
                        ("ptrRaw", ctypes.c_void_p),
                        ("ptr8", ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_ubyte)),
                        ("ptr16", ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_ushort))]

            if sys.platform == 'win32':
                cls.__ModelV2._fields_ = [                     # camera model v2 win32
                        ('name', ctypes.c_wchar_p),            # model name, in Windows, we use unicode
                        ('flag', ctypes.c_ulonglong),          # MIICAM_FLAG_xxx, 64 bits
                        ('maxspeed', ctypes.c_uint),           # number of speed level, same as Miicam_get_MaxSpeed(), the speed range = [0, maxspeed], closed interval
                        ('preview', ctypes.c_uint),            # number of preview resolution, same as Miicam_get_ResolutionNumber()
                        ('still', ctypes.c_uint),              # number of still resolution, same as Miicam_get_StillResolutionNumber()
                        ('maxfanspeed', ctypes.c_uint),        # maximum fan speed, fan speed range = [0, max], closed interval
                        ('ioctrol', ctypes.c_uint),            # number of input/output control
                        ('xpixsz', ctypes.c_float),            # physical pixel size in micrometer
                        ('ypixsz', ctypes.c_float),            # physical pixel size in micrometer
                        ('res', cls.__Resolution * 16)]
                cls.__DeviceV2._fields_ = [                    # win32
                        ('displayname', ctypes.c_wchar * 64),  # display name
                        ('id', ctypes.c_wchar * 64),           # unique and opaque id of a connected camera, for Miicam_Open
                        ('model', ctypes.POINTER(cls.__ModelV2))]
            else:
                cls.__ModelV2._fields_ = [                     # camera model v2 linux/mac
                        ('name', ctypes.c_char_p),             # model name
                        ('flag', ctypes.c_ulonglong),          # MIICAM_FLAG_xxx, 64 bits
                        ('maxspeed', ctypes.c_uint),           # number of speed level, same as Miicam_get_MaxSpeed(), the speed range = [0, maxspeed], closed interval
                        ('preview', ctypes.c_uint),            # number of preview resolution, same as Miicam_get_ResolutionNumber()
                        ('still', ctypes.c_uint),              # number of still resolution, same as Miicam_get_StillResolutionNumber()
                        ('maxfanspeed', ctypes.c_uint),        # maximum fan speed
                        ('ioctrol', ctypes.c_uint),            # number of input/output control
                        ('xpixsz', ctypes.c_float),            # physical pixel size in micrometer
                        ('ypixsz', ctypes.c_float),            # physical pixel size in micrometer
                        ('res', cls.__Resolution * 16)]
                cls.__DeviceV2._fields_ = [                    # linux/mac
                        ('displayname', ctypes.c_char * 64),   # display name
                        ('id', ctypes.c_char * 64),            # unique and opaque id of a connected camera, for Miicam_Open
                        ('model', ctypes.POINTER(cls.__ModelV2))]

            cls.__lib.Miicam_Version.argtypes = None
            cls.__lib.Miicam_EnumV2.restype = ctypes.c_uint
            cls.__lib.Miicam_EnumV2.argtypes = [cls.__DeviceV2 * MIICAM_MAX]
            cls.__lib.Miicam_EnumWithName.restype = ctypes.c_uint
            cls.__lib.Miicam_EnumWithName.argtypes = [cls.__DeviceV2 * MIICAM_MAX]
            cls.__lib.Miicam_put_Name.restype = ctypes.c_int
            cls.__lib.Miicam_get_Name.restype = ctypes.c_int
            cls.__lib.Miicam_Open.restype = ctypes.c_void_p
            cls.__lib.Miicam_Replug.restype = ctypes.c_int
            cls.__lib.Miicam_Update.restype = ctypes.c_int
            if sys.platform == 'win32':
                cls.__lib.Miicam_Version.restype = ctypes.c_wchar_p
                cls.__lib.Miicam_put_Name.argtypes = [ctypes.c_wchar_p, ctypes.c_char_p]
                cls.__lib.Miicam_get_Name.argtypes = [ctypes.c_wchar_p, ctypes.c_char * 64]
                cls.__lib.Miicam_Open.argtypes = [ctypes.c_wchar_p]
                cls.__lib.Miicam_Replug.argtypes = [ctypes.c_wchar_p]
                cls.__lib.Miicam_Update.argtypes = [ctypes.c_wchar_p, ctypes.c_wchar_p, cls.__PROGRESS_CALLBACK, ctypes.py_object]
            else:
                cls.__lib.Miicam_Version.restype = ctypes.c_char_p
                cls.__lib.Miicam_put_Name.argtypes = [ctypes.c_char_p, ctypes.c_char_p]
                cls.__lib.Miicam_get_Name.argtypes = [ctypes.c_char_p, ctypes.c_char * 64]
                cls.__lib.Miicam_Open.argtypes = [ctypes.c_char_p]
                cls.__lib.Miicam_Replug.argtypes = [ctypes.c_char_p]
                cls.__lib.Miicam_Update.argtypes = [ctypes.c_char_p, ctypes.c_char_p, cls.__PROGRESS_CALLBACK, ctypes.py_object]
            cls.__lib.Miicam_put_Name.errcheck = cls.__errcheck
            cls.__lib.Miicam_get_Name.errcheck = cls.__errcheck
            cls.__lib.Miicam_Replug.errcheck = cls.__errcheck
            cls.__lib.Miicam_Update.errcheck = cls.__errcheck
            cls.__lib.Miicam_set_Name.restype = ctypes.c_int
            cls.__lib.Miicam_set_Name.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_char_p]
            cls.__lib.Miicam_set_Name.errcheck = cls.__errcheck
            cls.__lib.Miicam_query_Name.restype = ctypes.c_int
            cls.__lib.Miicam_query_Name.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_char * 64]
            cls.__lib.Miicam_query_Name.errcheck = cls.__errcheck
            cls.__lib.Miicam_OpenByIndex.restype = ctypes.c_void_p
            cls.__lib.Miicam_OpenByIndex.argtypes = [ctypes.c_uint]
            cls.__lib.Miicam_Close.restype = None
            cls.__lib.Miicam_Close.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p]
            cls.__lib.Miicam_StartPullModeWithCallback.restype = ctypes.c_int
            cls.__lib.Miicam_StartPullModeWithCallback.errcheck = cls.__errcheck
            cls.__lib.Miicam_StartPullModeWithCallback.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p, cls.__EVENT_CALLBACK, ctypes.py_object]
            cls.__lib.Miicam_PullImagePtr.restype = ctypes.c_int
            cls.__lib.Miicam_PullImagePtr.errcheck = cls.__errcheck
